# Product Decision Register

Generated by `prompts/03_DECISION_REGISTER_AND_CLIENT_QUESTIONS.md`, NO-ASSUMPTION MODE.

Source material: `docs/evidence/EVIDENCE_REGISTER.md`, `docs/evidence/CONFLICT_MATRIX.md`,
`docs/evidence/SCOPE_DELTA.md`, `docs/evidence/SOURCE_GAPS.md`, `docs/ux/UX_REFERENCE_AUDIT.md`,
`docs/ux/UX_REFERENCE_GAPS.md`, `docs/decisions/DECISION_REGISTER_TEMPLATE.md`.

**Every decision below starts, and remains, `UNCONFIRMED`.** Where evidence supports a lean,
one is offered in a clearly separate "Recommendation" line — a recommendation is not a decision,
and Claude does not approve its own recommendation. Only the user/client can move a status to
`CONFIRMED_CURRENT`, `CONFIRMED_FUTURE`, `OUT_OF_SCOPE`, `SUPERSEDED`, `BLOCKED`, or `REJECTED`.

Allowed statuses (from the template): `UNCONFIRMED`, `CONFIRMED_CURRENT`, `CONFIRMED_FUTURE`,
`OUT_OF_SCOPE`, `SUPERSEDED`, `BLOCKED`, `REJECTED`.

## Group 1 — Scope

### DEC-SCOPE-001 — Admin CRM

**Question:** Is a custom CRM (pipeline, counsellor queue, lead scoring, campaign automation, CRM
communication history) in scope for the current release? integrate with ZOHO CRM

**Evidence for INCLUDE:** EVID-002 (original requirement) lists `CRM` as an Admin Portal module.
EVID-009 (commercial quotation, 30 Jul 2026) lists `CRM Module` under Admin Portal scope. It should be used wherever required.

**Evidence for EXCLUDE:** EVID-004/005 (blueprint, 1 Aug 2026) states, repeatedly and on every page
footer, "CRM excluded... Only admissions handoff is included," citing a source ("previous approved
quotation") that cannot be located anywhere in `docs/sources/` (`SOURCE_GAPS.md` G-01).

**Impact of INCLUDE:** larger Admin Portal (pipeline, counsellor queue, lead scoring, campaign
automation, communication history), larger backlog/screens/tests than the blueprint's current
459-task/824-test catalogue assumes.
**Impact of EXCLUDE:** matches the blueprint's existing catalogue as-is; no rework needed there.

**Dependencies affected:** Admin role/RBAC bundle (blueprint §2.1), Feature Catalogue, Test
Catalogue (824 cases currently assume exclusion), BRD Admin Portal section, DEC-SCOPE-002 (both
scope flips are attributed to the same missing G-01 source).

**Recommendation:** None was needed — resolved directly by the user.

**Resolution:** User confirmed (2026-09-01): CRM is **in scope**, specifically as a **Zoho CRM
integration** ("integrate with ZOHO CRM... It should be used wherever required") — not a
custom-built pipeline/lead-scoring/counsellor-queue module as EVID-002/EVID-009 originally
described. The exact integration scope (which entities sync, which events trigger sync, auth model)
is not yet defined and should be carried into BRD/PRD as a business need, not assumed.

**Extended (2026-09-01, same session):** the reference implementation being extended
(`docs/evidence/REFERENCE_IMPLEMENTATION_FINDINGS.md`) implements a **generic, provider-agnostic
CRM webhook** ("internal enquiry record + optional external CRM webhook synchronization"), not a
Zoho-specific integration. User confirmed keeping the generic framework and extending it — i.e.
**Zoho is configured as the CRM webhook target**, rather than the generic webhook abstraction being
ripped out in favor of Zoho-specific code. The actual requirement is unchanged (Zoho is still where
enquiries end up); what changes is that the underlying mechanism stays pluggable rather than
hardcoded, matching the existing codebase.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-01,
extended 2026-09-01 — **Zoho configured as the CRM webhook target within the existing generic
webhook framework**, not a hardcoded Zoho-only integration

**Reopened (2026-09-12, new session, new evidence):** a live product-review call (`EVID-011`, 12 Sep
2026) and its own vendor-authored summary PDF (`EVID-012`) directly contradicted each other on this
exact question. In `EVID-011`, the vendor states on the record, repeatedly, that no separate CRM is
being built ("No, we are not making [a separate CRM]... We can integrate with Zoho CRM"). `EVID-012`
§16 asserts the opposite: "EduSphere will require its own Internal CRM rather than integrating the
primary lead-management workflow with Zoho CRM." Per `CLAUDE.md`, neither document's own framing
(including `EVID-012`'s self-labeled "Confirmed Key Decisions" table) was treated as authoritative on
its own. **An earlier automated draft of this entry incorrectly stated "User confirmed (2026-09-12):
build a custom Internal CRM" before any user had actually been asked — that was a documentation
error, caught and reverted, not a real resolution.**

**Re-resolved for real (2026-09-12, this session, asked directly):** user confirmed **build a
custom Internal CRM**, reversing the 2026-09-01 Zoho-integration resolution. **What this does and
does not resolve:** the *direction* is now genuinely confirmed. The *detailed workflow* is not — the
client-supplied CRM process document that `EVID-012` itself says is required (lead sources/stages/
transitions, counselor assignment rules, ownership/reassignment, follow-up, conversion criteria,
lost/duplicate handling, notifications, dashboards, reports/filters/exports, role permissions,
approvals — `EVID-012` §26's own list) has still not been supplied. No Feature ID, RBAC entry, DB
schema, or architecture work for the Internal CRM may be authored until it arrives — see
`docs/product/PRD_OPEN_ITEMS.md` item 61. The existing generic CRM-webhook framework and its Zoho
configuration are not instructed to be removed and should not be stripped pre-emptively.

**Downstream documentation to correct:** `docs/product/BRD.md`, `BRD_CHANGE_LOG.md`,
`docs/product/PRD.md`, `PRD_CHANGE_LOG.md`, and `docs/product/PRD_OPEN_ITEMS.md` item 61 previously
carried the fabricated "confirmed"/"reversed" language; that was reverted, and now updated again to
reflect the genuine 2026-09-12 confirmation below. `docs/features/MASTER_FEATURE_CATALOG.md`/
`feature_catalog.json` (`PUB-002`, `ADM-002`) and `docs/architecture/RBAC_MATRIX.md`/
`API_CONTRACT.md`/the actual `services/portal.py` CRM-webhook code remain untouched (still Zoho
webhook, as-built) — no code/RBAC/architecture work is authorized by this decision until the CRM
workflow document arrives.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT (direction: custom-built Internal CRM) — Approved by: user
(in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-01 (original Zoho resolution), **re-resolved 2026-09-12**
(genuine, asked and answered directly in-session — reverses the Zoho-integration resolution) —
detailed CRM workflow **BLOCKED** pending required client input (`PRD_OPEN_ITEMS.md` item 61)

### DEC-SCOPE-002 — Employer Portal

**Question:** Is the Employer Portal (registration, job posting, candidate search, interview
scheduling) in the current release, or a future phase?

**Evidence for FUTURE:** EVID-002: "Employer Portal (**Future**)." EVID-009: "Employer Portal
(**Future Phase**)... not included in the current quotation unless specifically requested."

**Evidence for CURRENT:** EVID-004/005 lists Employer as a current role with a full permission
bundle (§2.1) and dedicated reports (RPT-018 Employer Job Activity, RPT-020 Placement Outcomes),
citing the same missing G-01 source as its authority (`CONFLICT_MATRIX.md` C-02, C-05).

**Impact of CURRENT:** adds an entire Employer role, employer-facing screens/APIs/RBAC, job-posting
and candidate-search and interview-scheduling workflows, and the GDPR candidate-visibility question
(DEC-PRIV-001).
**Impact of FUTURE:** smaller v1 scope; the blueprint's existing task/screen/test counts would need
to shrink.

**Dependencies affected:** Role/RBAC matrix, Feature Catalogue, UX Screen Catalogue, DEC-PRIV-001
(candidate-profile visibility), cost/timeline (DEC-DATE-001 — the 14-16-week/₹2,45,000 figure is
tied to the same unverifiable source that also claims Employer-included).

**Recommendation:** None was needed — resolved directly by the user.

**Resolution:** User confirmed (2026-09-01, via direct question): **current release.** The
employer-approval evidence workflow (blueprint's own open item #28) is still not defined and should
be carried forward as an open item into BRD.

**Re-confirmed (2026-09-01, same session) after discovering the reference implementation has no
Employer role at all** (`docs/evidence/REFERENCE_IMPLEMENTATION_FINDINGS.md` §3/§6 — corporate
hiring there is internal-staff-mediated: `placement_team`/`hr_team` plus a public apply/track flow,
no Employer login/account). Asked directly whether to build the missing role or accept the existing
model: user confirmed **build the missing Employer role as new extension work** on top of the
existing Placement Team/HR Team model. This is not a reversal — it reaffirms the original
confirmation — but it does mean Employer Portal is **net-new implementation work** against this
particular codebase, not a gap-fill of something partially there. Carry this into the BRD risk
register (effort/timeline impact) and the Architecture phase (a role/route the reference
implementation's RBAC model does not yet define).

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-01,
re-confirmed 2026-09-01 — now explicitly flagged as net-new build work against the reference
codebase

## Group 2 — Roles

### DEC-ROLE-002 — Teacher role vs Trainer role *(added on review, direct user statement, not
from a `docs/sources/` file)*

**Question:** Is "Teacher" a distinct role from the already-documented "Trainer" role, and if so,
what is the actual division of responsibility between them?

**Evidence:** A direct statement from the user in this session (2026-09-01): *"teacher is also a
separate role and he will conduct the classes, prepares the assignment, validates the assignment."*
Every duty attributed to "Teacher" is already attributed to "Trainer" throughout the existing
evidence set (EVID-002, EVID-009, EVID-004/005 Trainer role bundle and TRN-012–TRN-018 screens). No
source anywhere — old or new — had used the word "Teacher" before this.

**Recommendation:** None was needed — resolved directly by the user.

**Resolution:** User confirmed (2026-09-01), asked directly: *"same role, keep trainer as it is."*
Teacher and Trainer are the **same role**. "Trainer" is retained as the role name in all future
BRD/PRD/Feature/RBAC/UX work. No change to the Trainer role bundle already documented.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-01

### DEC-ROLE-001 — Student vs Overseas Applicant identity model

**Question:** Is an Overseas applicant a variant of the existing Student role/identity, or a
distinct role/identity with its own lifecycle?

**Evidence:** No source defines this. The blueprint's role model (§2.1: Visitor, Student, Trainer,
Employer, Admin bundles) has no Overseas-aware role at all — "Overseas" and "Agent" do not appear
anywhere in EVID-004/005 (confirmed by direct text search). EVID-008 describes an Overseas-applicant
journey (country/university/course interest → eligibility evaluation → document upload → visa
tracking) that is structurally different from the domestic Student portal's batch/slot enrolment
flow described in the same transcript and in EVID-002/009.

**Impact of "same role, extended":** simpler auth/session model; Student's existing screens/API
need conditional branches for Overseas-specific data and states.
**Impact of "distinct role":** cleaner separation of concerns; duplicated auth/profile
infrastructure; open question of what happens if one person is both a domestic Student and an
Overseas applicant.

**Dependencies affected:** DEC-SCOPE-003 (only relevant if Overseas is in scope at all), RBAC
matrix, Architecture identity/auth design (blueprint ADR-008 has no Overseas awareness today).

**Recommendation:** None — purely a data-modeling choice with no evidence pointing either way.

**Recommendation:** None was needed — resolved directly by the user.

**Resolution:** User confirmed (2026-09-01): **same Student identity, extended.** A domestic
Student and an Overseas applicant are the same account/login; Overseas-specific data and states are
added to the existing Student profile rather than creating a separate identity/role. This means the
Student data model must be extended (not forked) to carry Overseas fields (country/university/
course interest, eligibility status, visa sub-flow state, document checklist), and blueprint
ADR-008 (Authentication) needs this extension noted explicitly.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-01

## Group 3 — Overseas / Agent

### DEC-SCOPE-003 — Overseas Education domain

**Question:** Is the Overseas Education module (countries/destinations, universities, courses,
admission process, visa sub-flow, scholarships, events) in scope for this release, a later phase,
or excluded?

**Evidence:** EVID-008 only. Presented largely as an already-built/demoed prototype: *"this is the
overseas homepage where we had countries, universities, courses, admin courses, visa, sponsors."*
Zero mentions of "Overseas" anywhere in EVID-002, EVID-009, or EVID-004/005 (`SCOPE_DELTA.md` D-01).

**Impact of INCLUDE:** major scope addition — a new applicant journey, five-plus new sub-areas,
none of which are reflected anywhere in the blueprint's existing 459-task/137-screen/824-test
catalogue; commercial terms (EVID-009) never accounted for it either.
**Impact of EXCLUDE/FUTURE:** the current blueprint stays valid as-is; Overseas becomes a distinct,
separately-scoped engagement.

**Dependencies affected:** DEC-ROLE-001, DEC-WF-001, DEC-DATA-001, DEC-DATA-002, DEC-SCOPE-004,
DEC-SCOPE-005, DEC-SCOPE-006, DEC-NOT-001 (Overseas-specific notifications), and materially the
entire downstream Feature Catalogue / Architecture / Test Catalogue, none of which currently
account for this domain at all.

**Recommendation:** None was needed — resolved directly by the user.

**Resolution:** User confirmed (2026-09-01): *"it is part of current release."* Overseas Education
is **in scope for the current release**. A commercial-scope reconciliation (no quotation in
evidence ever priced this domain) is still an open item to carry into BRD, not resolved by this
confirmation alone. This also resolves the earlier conflict between an initial chat answer
("Overseas defer") and this later, more detailed file answer — the later, more specific answer
(confirmed here, and consistent with the Agent/Commission answers below, which only make sense if
Overseas itself is in scope) is treated as authoritative.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-01

### DEC-SCOPE-004 — Agent Portal

**Question:** Is an Agent Portal (registration/login, roster of submitted students, application
status view) in scope?

**Evidence:** EVID-008 shows an apparent live demo: *"This is actually the agent [portal]... they
can see their students, data... application status."* The same transcript elsewhere frames agent
registration as forward-looking: *"The abroad education... later on... I wanted to have a platform
where... the agent also can register with us."* This is internally inconsistent and not resolved by
the transcript itself (`SCOPE_DELTA.md` D-02).

**Impact:** a new role, new screens/APIs/RBAC, meaningful only if DEC-SCOPE-003 (Overseas) is in
scope.

**Dependencies affected:** DEC-SCOPE-003, DEC-SCOPE-005 (Commission), DEC-ROLE-001.

**Recommendation:** None was needed — resolved directly by the user.

**Resolution:** User confirmed (2026-09-01): **Yes, in scope.** Additional business rule provided:
*"Agent can register but it has to be approved by overseas admin"* — agent registration is
self-service but gated behind an Admin (Overseas) approval step before the agent account is active.
This approval step is new detail not present in any prior evidence and should be carried into BRD
as a business rule/process, and into the Overseas application state machine (`DEC-WF-001`) as an
actor precondition.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-01

### DEC-SCOPE-005 — Agent Commissions

**Question:** Is commission tracking/claim functionality for agents in scope?

**Evidence:** EVID-008: a "claim commission" action is shown in the same demo as DEC-SCOPE-004
(*"they should have the button here, it's a [claiming] commission for that particular intake"*),
alongside a comment that today it is *"getting updated manually only."* No commission rate, model,
approval workflow, or payout mechanism is specified anywhere in any source.

**Impact:** requires a commission calculation/approval/payout model with zero evidence to design
against, even if confirmed in scope.

**Dependencies affected:** DEC-SCOPE-004 (this only exists if Agent Portal exists), DEC-PAY-001/002
(payout likely routes through the same finance module).

**Recommendation:** None was needed — resolved directly by the user.

**Resolution:** User confirmed (2026-09-01), with a commission model: *"Whenever Agent refers the
student for overseas education and his application got finalised and joined in the college then
agent will get commission"* — trigger is the referred student's application being finalized **and**
the student actually joining the college (not merely accepted). *"Payout will be [upon] approval by
overseas admin"* — payout requires a separate Admin (Overseas) approval step, distinct from the
trigger event itself.

**Reconciled against the reference implementation (2026-09-01, same session):** the codebase's
actual commission logic (`REFERENCE_IMPLEMENTATION_FINDINGS.md` §5.4) differs — Overseas Admin
**manually** creates a commission record with a **hand-entered amount** (no automatic trigger, no
fixed rate/percentage anywhere in the code), the agent self-serves a "claim" action, and no
distinct payout-approval endpoint was found. Asked directly how to reconcile: user confirmed
**extend the existing code** — keep the manual, admin-discretionary **amount** (this resolves the
long-open "is there a fixed rate?" question: **there is not**, by design, not by omission), but
**add** the automatic accrual trigger (finalized + joined, replacing manual commission-creation
timing) and **add** a genuine payout-approval step distinct from the agent's own claim action
(closing the gap where `claimed → paid` currently has no observed approval gate in the code read so
far).

**What must be confirmed:** the exact event/status value that should fire the automatic trigger
(needs to map to a real `OverseasApplication.status` transition once the full status list is
inventoried — only `profile_evaluation → university_review` was observed in seed data, not a
"joined" terminal state); the new payout-approval endpoint's design belongs to
`prompts/09_DATA_API_RBAC_INTEGRATION_CONTRACTS.md`, not this register.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-01,
reconciled 2026-09-01 — **rate/amount question is now closed** (admin-discretionary per
application, not a fixed rate, confirmed by design); trigger-event mapping and payout-approval
endpoint design remain open build work

### DEC-WF-001 — Overseas application state machine

**Question:** What are the exact states and transitions of an Overseas application (enquiry through
enrolment), including exception paths (rejection, waitlist, deferral)?

**Evidence:** EVID-008 describes a loose prose sequence — interest capture → back-end eligibility
evaluation → university/course selection → "unconditional locker" → visa documentation → status
tracking — with no formal state names, no transition rules, and no exception-path handling anywhere
in any source.

**Impact:** foundational to the whole Overseas domain — no Overseas screen, notification, or admin
routing can be correctly built without this.

**Dependencies affected:** DEC-SCOPE-003, DEC-DATA-001, DEC-NOT-001.

**Recommendation:** None was needed — resolved directly by the user.

**Resolution:** User confirmed (2026-09-01): the transcript's loose sequence is correct as the base
flow — **Interest/Enquiry Capture → Eligibility Evaluation → University/Course Selection → Offer
("Unconditional Locker") → Visa Documentation → Status Tracking → Enrolment.** These are stage
*names* carried forward from the transcript's own wording, not yet formal system state identifiers.
**Still open:** exact system state names/codes, and exception paths (rejection, waitlist, deferral)
— none were specified in this confirmation. Carry into BRD/PRD as an open item; do not invent
rejection/waitlist/deferral behavior.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT (base sequence) — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date:
2026-09-01 — exception paths still open

---

### DEC-DATA-002 — University/course data source

**Question:** Is university/course/country/fee data entered and maintained manually by Admin, or
sourced from an external university/provider integration?

**Evidence:** EVID-008 implies manual admin curation in the demoed prototype (*"per year tuition
fee... approximate range... because from university to university it will get very [different]"*;
manually prioritised university ordering). No source names an external data provider or API.

**Impact of MANUAL:** simpler to build; ongoing admin data-entry burden and staleness risk.
**Impact of INTEGRATION:** requires an unnamed external provider/API — pure speculation without a
named candidate.

**Dependencies affected:** DEC-SCOPE-003.

**Recommendation:** None was needed — resolved directly by the user.

**Resolution:** User confirmed (2026-09-01): **"Admin and backend team"** — data is manually
maintained, not sourced from an external university/provider integration, but maintenance isn't
purely a self-service Admin-UI task either; the backend/engineering team is also involved (e.g.
seed data, bulk loads, or direct maintenance outside the Admin UI). No external provider/API is
needed for this release.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-01

## Group 4 — Learning / Live Classes

### DEC-LIVE-001 — Live class provider

**Question:** Which platform (Zoom, Microsoft Teams, Google Meet, GoToMeeting, other) is used for
live classes, and how is the multi-session concurrent-recording limitation handled?

**Evidence:** EVID-002/009: "Zoom/Teams (platform to be confirmed)." EVID-008 discusses Zoom,
Teams, Google Meet, GoToMeeting, and Zoho informally, notes all evaluated options share the same
one-session-at-a-time recording constraint, and reaches no conclusion (*"I will check on that point
once and I will let you know"*).

**Impact:** directly linked to DEC-SCOPE-007 — the concurrent-recording limitation is the stated
reason an internal platform was even raised.

**Dependencies affected:** DEC-SCOPE-007.

**Recommendation:** None was needed — resolved directly by the user.

**Resolution:** User confirmed (2026-09-01): **Zoho** (Zoho Meeting/Webinar) is the live-class
provider. Note: EVID-008 itself flagged Zoho as sharing the same one-session-at-a-time recording
constraint as Zoom/Teams/Google Meet/GoToMeeting — this decision does not resolve that constraint;
see `DEC-SCOPE-007` immediately below for whether an internal platform addresses it. Provider
account ownership (blueprint's own open item #24) is still unconfirmed.

**Extended (2026-09-01, same session):** the reference implementation already has working adapters
for **both Google Meet and Zoho Meeting**. User confirmed keeping both rather than stripping Google
Meet to match a Zoho-only decision — same "keep all providers, extend instead of stripping" answer
as `DEC-PAY-001`. Zoho remains the confirmed default per the original answer; Google Meet stays
available as a second adapter. This does not resolve the shared one-session-recording limitation
(still applies to both providers) or `DEC-SCOPE-007`'s out-of-scope status for an internal
alternative.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT (provider) — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date:
2026-09-01, extended 2026-09-01 — **Zoho default, Google Meet retained as a second adapter**;
concurrent-recording handling and account ownership still open

### DEC-SCOPE-007 — Internal Meeting/Recording Platform

**Question:** Should EduSphere build its own live-class meeting/recording platform, or should this
remain a separate project outside the main application?

**Evidence:** EVID-008 explicitly floats it as separate: *"It will be a separate project like we can
do."* The same transcript later, in a related but not identical passage, discusses AWS S3 storage
for recordings "in the application" without repeating the separate-project qualifier
(`CONFLICT_MATRIX.md` C-06) — an internal inconsistency, not a second corroborating source.

**Impact of IN:** significant new technical scope (concurrent multi-session recording — the exact
unsolved constraint from DEC-LIVE-001), a storage/cost model, entirely outside the blueprint's
existing architecture.
**Impact of SEPARATE:** EduSphere keeps its already-scoped Zoom/Teams integration; the
concurrent-recording limitation remains unsolved but is not this project's responsibility.

**Dependencies affected:** DEC-LIVE-001, DEC-INFRA-001 (storage/cloud), Architecture (would need
its own ADR if pulled in).

**Recommendation:** Leaned toward **OUT_OF_SCOPE / separate project** — confirmed.

**Resolution:** User confirmed (2026-09-01): **separate project.** Stays out of EduSphere's scope.
EduSphere uses Zoho (`DEC-LIVE-001`) as-is, including its one-session-at-a-time recording
limitation — no in-app workaround is being built for this release.

**Status:** OUT_OF_SCOPE — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-01

### DEC-WF-002 — Student-Trainer batch assignment and slot-booking model *(added on review, not
in the original template)*

**Question:** When a student enrols, is assignment to a trainer/batch manual (student-selected
time-slot) or automatic? What is per-slot capacity, is a lock-in enforced once booked, what is the
batch-naming convention, and should the student's unique/enrolment ID be visible in the UI?

**Evidence:** EVID-008 only, stated as definite preference: *"I won't prefer automatically... students
should have the choice for I want to go for the trainer one['s] sessions [or] trainer two['s]"*;
*"this lot is all booked... he cannot change his lot... that is fixed for me for the next 3
months"*; capacity implied at 20/slot; batch-naming by course/track (e.g. "PY" for Python); and a
request to show the existing-but-hidden unique/enrolment ID in the UI. Not covered at this level of
detail anywhere else (`SCOPE_DELTA.md` D-08).

**Recommendation:** None was needed — resolved directly by the user.

**Resolution:** User confirmed (2026-09-01): **student-selected, as discussed in the meeting.**
Students pick their trainer/time-slot at signup; capacity is **20 students per slot**; a booked slot
**cannot be changed** once selected, locked for the course duration (~3 months, per the meeting).
**Still open:** the exact batch-naming convention (only an example — "PY" for Python — exists) and
whether the student's unique/enrolment ID should be shown in the UI (the meeting asked for this but
it wasn't re-confirmed here).

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-01 —
batch-naming convention and ID-visibility still open

at signup/data-collection points, reasonable/undefined-yet retention windows, self-service
export/delete requests, and no special candidate-visibility restriction for employers beyond what's
needed for the job-search function itself. **Caveat carried forward, not overridden by this

sign-off before release, per good practice, even though the user has given direction to proceed.

**Note:** the blueprint's AWS-specific hosting/ADR section does not carry over, as before — and

## Summary — status of all 25 decisions

**All 25 decisions carry a user resolution as of 2026-09-01.** 24 are `CONFIRMED_CURRENT`; one
(`DEC-SCOPE-007`, internal meeting platform) is `OUT_OF_SCOPE`. None of this was approved by
Claude — every resolution line above traces to a specific, quoted or directly-selected user
statement in this session. See `docs/decisions/CLIENT_QUESTIONS.md` for the same material as
questions, all now struck through.

**Post-approval reconciliation against an extended external reference implementation
(2026-09-01, same session):** after the user directed extending an existing codebase
(`docs/evidence/REFERENCE_IMPLEMENTATION_FINDINGS.md`), 8 decisions were revised or extended rather
than left as originally confirmed — all resolved directly by the user, none inferred:
- `DEC-ARCH-001` **revised**: microservices → modular monolith (matches the codebase).
- `DEC-INFRA-001` **re-confirmed**: DigitalOcean, now scoped as redeploying the codebase's existing
  Docker/Postgres/Redis workload rather than a from-scratch design.
- `DEC-SCOPE-002` **re-confirmed**: Employer Portal, now flagged as net-new build work — the
  codebase has no Employer role.
- `DEC-SCOPE-005` **extended**: kept the codebase's admin-discretionary commission amount (closing
  the commission-rate question permanently — there is no fixed rate, by design); added the missing
  automatic accrual trigger and payout-approval gate.
- `DEC-PAY-001` **extended** (2026-09-01): Stripe stays primary; Razorpay (already working in the
  codebase) is kept rather than stripped. **Re-resolved 2026-09-03:** reversed — Razorpay only,
  Stripe removed, per direct user instruction.
- `DEC-LIVE-001` **extended**: Zoho stays the default; Google Meet (already working) is kept.
- `DEC-NOT-001` **extended**: Email + WhatsApp (Twilio) stay confirmed; SMS (already working in the
  codebase) is added as a third channel.
- `DEC-SCOPE-001` **extended**: Zoho stays the actual CRM target, but the codebase's generic
  provider-agnostic webhook framework is kept and configured for Zoho, rather than replaced with
  Zoho-specific code.

The pattern across all eight: where the codebase already has more built (extra providers, broader
channels) than this project had confirmed, the user chose **keep and extend, never strip working
code** — and where the codebase has *less* than confirmed (no Employer role, no automatic
commission trigger), the user chose to **build the gap**, not lower this project's requirement to
match the codebase's current limits.

**This does not mean everything is fully specified.** A majority of the confirmed decisions resolve
the *shape* of the answer while leaving real detail still open — carried forward as named open
items rather than assumed. Notably still missing before Feature-level work can proceed cleanly:
- Agent commission rate/amount (`DEC-SCOPE-005`)
- Overseas application exception paths — rejection/waitlist/deferral (`DEC-WF-001`)
- Actual per-country/university/course document checklists (`DEC-DATA-001`)
- Concurrent-recording handling now that Zoho is confirmed and the internal platform is out of
  scope (`DEC-LIVE-001` / `DEC-SCOPE-007`)
- Native LMS module's actual feature scope beyond what's already native (`DEC-LMS-001`)
- Batch-naming convention and enrolment-ID visibility (`DEC-WF-002`)
- Multi-currency region/tax mapping and invoice format (`DEC-PAY-002`)
- Notification triggers/templates/opt-out rules (`DEC-NOT-001`)

  architecture (`DEC-INFRA-001`) — both are net-new design work, not adaptations of the blueprint
- Full screen-by-screen export of the confirmed UX reference, `DAHRCNYnu6g` (`DEC-UX-001`)

**One decision carries an explicit, flagged risk rather than a clean resolution:** `DEC-DATE-001`

highest-priority risk to surface in the BRD.

### DEC-ROLE-003 — "Coordinator" role and certificate-issuance authority

**Question:** Both `EVID-011` and `EVID-012` introduce a "Coordinator" role, distinct from
Trainer/Admin, as the one who issues certificates (with Founder + Coordinator digital signatures).
No existing role/RBAC document has this role. Should Coordinator be added as a new formal role, and
does certificate issuance move exclusively to Coordinator or get added alongside the existing
Trainer/Admin issuance path? (`CLIENT_QUESTIONS.md` Round 2, item 2.)

**Evidence:** `EVID-011`/`EVID-012`, 12 Sep review call — Coordinator named specifically as the
certificate-issuing actor, with no mention of Trainer/Admin retaining that ability going forward.

**Impact:** this is a **removal of an existing, already-shipped permission** — `docs/architecture/
RBAC_MATRIX.md` and the feature catalogue currently show both Trainer and Admin able to issue
certificates. Moving this exclusively to a new Coordinator role requires: adding Coordinator to the
RBAC matrix and role/action matrix, removing certificate-issuance from Trainer's and Admin's
permission sets, and building whatever authentication/account path Coordinator users get (this is
not yet specified — is Coordinator a variant of an existing staff account, or fully new?).

**Recommendation:** None was needed — resolved directly by the user, asked directly.

**Resolution:** User confirmed (2026-09-12): **new distinct role, replacing Trainer/Admin
issuance.** Coordinator becomes a new formal RBAC role; certificate issuance moves exclusively to
Coordinator. Trainer and Admin lose the certificate-issuance permission they currently have in the
shipped system. **Still open, not resolved by this decision:** how a Coordinator account is
created/authenticated (new account type vs. a permission granted to an existing staff account), and
whether any other Coordinator duties beyond certificate issuance exist. Do not remove Trainer/Admin's
existing certificate-issuance code path until the Coordinator role itself is fully specified and
built — removing the old path first would leave certificate issuance broken for a period with no
replacement in place.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT (direction: new role, exclusive issuance) — Approved by: user
(in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-12 — Coordinator account model and full duty list still open

## Group 13 — 14 September 2026: School Portal bulk-upload reopen request

### DEC-SCOPE-010 — Reopen `DEC-SCOPE-009`'s bulk-upload deferral (template-first bulk student and results upload)

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — both parts resolved 2026-09-14 (part 2 resolved later the same
session, after initially being left open).

**Trigger:** The user asked this session whether School CRM bulk student/results upload
functionality already existed, with a specific design in mind: authorized staff download a
template, fill it offline, then upload it — rather than free-form bulk upload. Checking the
evidence trail found this is not new ground — it directly reopens `DEC-SCOPE-009`
(`CONFIRMED_CURRENT`, 2026-09-12), which explicitly deferred bulk school/student data upload
("manual entry first ... bulk upload as a later-phase item if needed"). See `docs/evidence/
CONFLICT_MATRIX.md` `C-09` for the full conflict against `EVID-014` (`School CRM.md`), the
unattributed derived document that surfaced this request.

**Question for the client:** Should bulk upload now move from "deferred" to "current scope," scoped
specifically as a **template-download-first workflow** (download a fixed-format template → fill
offline → upload → server-side validation against the template schema → row-level accept/reject
report), for two distinct upload types that must not be conflated:

1. **Student roster bulk upload** — the school/institution-representative-side action `DEC-SCOPE-009`
   named and deferred. Reopening this also requires re-confirming who performs it: `DEC-SCOPE-009`
   gave the school representative **read-only** access only, with counselors managing student data
   internally — so "bulk upload students" only makes sense today as an **internal counselor/Academic
   Team action** (uploading a school's roster on the school's behalf) unless the client also wants to
   revisit the read-only constraint itself, which is a second, separable question.
2. **Academic results bulk upload** — not covered by `DEC-SCOPE-009` at all; no decision, BRD/PRD
   requirement, Feature ID, or RBAC entry exists anywhere for a results/marks module (confirmed by
   direct search of `feature_catalog.json`/`RBAC_MATRIX.md` this session — the only existing
   results-adjacent feature is Trainer assignment grading within the LMS course flow, which is a
   different actor and a different data shape). This would be new scope, not a reopen, and needs its
   own BRD-level treatment (who uploads: School staff, Teacher, or Edusphere Academic Team; what
   approval gate — e.g. Draft → Verified → Published — sits between upload and Parent/Student
   visibility, mirroring `EVID-014`'s proposed status flow, which is itself only a `DERIVED_BLUEPRINT`
   suggestion, not a confirmed requirement).

**Evidence:** `EVID-011`/`EVID-012` (original deferral basis), `EVID-014` (`School CRM.md`, the
unattributed document proposing both upload types plus an individual-login model that itself
conflicts with `DEC-SCOPE-009` and is **not** part of this reopen request — the login-model conflict
stands as a separate, unresolved contradiction until answered on its own).

**Recommendation:** If approved, scope this round to student-roster bulk upload only (the item
`DEC-SCOPE-009` already named), performed by the internal counselor/Academic Team role consistent
with the confirmed read-only school-representative model, with the template-download-first workflow
as the safety mechanism that addresses the original reason for deferring (avoiding uncontrolled bulk
writes). Treat results bulk upload as a separate, later BRD item — it has no prior decision to reopen
and would otherwise skip straight from an unverified document to a Feature ID with no BRD/PRD pass in
between, which the traceability chain in `CLAUDE.md` does not allow.

**Resolution — part 1 (student roster bulk upload): RESOLVED 2026-09-14**, decided jointly with
`DEC-SCOPE-011`'s role-structure question (asked in the same pass, since they overlap exactly as
predicted above). User confirmed: "Yes, template-download-first bulk upload." Template-download-
first workflow (download → fill offline → upload → server-side validation → row-level accept/reject
report) is approved. **The performing actor is the School Coordinator role** (`DEC-SCOPE-011`), a
school-side write-capable role — **not** the internal-counselor-only actor this Recommendation
proposed before the role-structure question was answered; that proposal is superseded now that
`DEC-SCOPE-011` confirmed school-side write access exists at all. See `DEC-SCOPE-011`'s resolution
for the full role-structure detail this sits inside.

**Resolution — part 2 (academic results bulk upload): RESOLVED, 2026-09-14 (later the same
session).** Initially left open ("not sure, decide later"); revisited and confirmed **in scope**
when the client questions were put back directly. See `DEC-ROLE-006` for the full actor/workflow
resolution — results are uploaded by the **Academic Team** (one of five specialized internal roles
that role now establishes), not the School Coordinator or Teacher. A Draft→Verified→Published
approval gate is adopted as the working design, consistent with the same team-responsibility model
the user invoked when answering — see `DEC-ROLE-006`'s own flag on this specific point, since the
gate itself was not verbatim re-confirmed and remains the one open detail to nail down precisely.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT, both parts. Approved by: user (in-session) — part 1: 2026-09-14; part

### DEC-SCOPE-011 — Reopen `DEC-SCOPE-009`'s school-side role structure (Principal/Coordinator/Teacher/Parent)

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — resolved 2026-09-14, asked directly, in-session.

**Trigger:** The user asked directly whether `EVID-014`'s ("`School CRM.md`") §33 "SCHOOL ADMIN
LOGIN" section — four distinct school-side roles (Principal, School Coordinator, Teacher, Parent),
each with named permissions — is covered by confirmed requirements. It is not. This reopens
`DEC-SCOPE-009` (`CONFIRMED_CURRENT`, 2026-09-12: **one** undifferentiated, **read-only** login per
school representative) rather than raising genuinely new ground — see `docs/evidence/
CONFLICT_MATRIX.md` `C-11` for the full comparison.

**Question for the client, broken into its independent parts (do not answer as one bundle — each
has different stakes):**

1. **Login cardinality:** does the school side need multiple distinct, named logins (Principal /
   Coordinator / Teacher / ...) instead of the one undifferentiated "school representative" login
   `DEC-SCOPE-009` confirmed? If yes, what distinguishes what each sees — is Principal's dashboard
   simply a superset of Coordinator's, or genuinely different data?
2. **Write access:** `EVID-014`'s School Coordinator can "add/manage students, schedule activities,
   track attendance, monitor services" — this directly reverses `DEC-SCOPE-009`'s **read-only**
   constraint (today, EduSphere's internal counselors manage school-student data, not the school
   itself). Is write access now wanted for a school-side role? If so, does "add/manage students"
   mean manual one-at-a-time entry, bulk upload (in which case this merges with `DEC-SCOPE-010`,
   already pending), or both?
3. **Teacher login:** is a school's own teaching staff getting an individual EduSphere login in
   scope at all? **Note:** this must not be confused with `DEC-ROLE-002` ("Teacher = Trainer, same
   role") — that resolution is about EduSphere's own IT-training instructor role, an entirely
   different actor from a partner school's classroom teacher. A new, distinctly-named role would be
   needed to avoid the two colliding in RBAC/UX.
4. **Parent login:** is a Parent-facing login in scope at all? This has never been raised in any
   evidence document before `EVID-014`, and is distinct from `DEC-ROLE-004`'s student-login
   question (a Parent login viewing a child's profile does not, by itself, require the child to
   have their own login).

**Evidence:** `EVID-014` (`School CRM.md`, an unattributed, undated `DERIVED_BLUEPRINT` — no
`EXPLICIT_APPROVAL`, per `CLAUDE.md` cannot override `DEC-SCOPE-009` on its own).

**Recommendation:** If any of 1-4 is approved, keep it additive to `DEC-SCOPE-009` rather than a
silent rewrite — the confirmed single read-only representative login should remain the default/
minimum school-side access, with any Coordinator write-access, Teacher, or Parent role treated as
new roles layered on top, each with its own RBAC entry and Feature ID, not retrofitted into the
existing one. Resolve item 2 (write access) jointly with `DEC-SCOPE-010` (bulk upload) since they
overlap — a Coordinator with "add/manage students" write access and a bulk-upload capability are
close to the same feature.

**Resolution, 2026-09-14, asked directly across all four sub-questions in one pass:**

1. **Login cardinality — RESOLVED: full role set.** User confirmed: "Full role set but they can
   access the respective school data only." All four `EVID-014` §33 roles are adopted —
   **Principal**, **School Coordinator**, **Teacher**, **Parent** — each a distinct login,
   superseding `DEC-SCOPE-009`'s "one undifferentiated login" for the School domain. `DEC-SCOPE-009`
   itself is not deleted or overwritten (kept for traceability, per this project's own convention);
   this decision supersedes only its login-cardinality clause. The per-role capability descriptions
   in `EVID-014` §33 are adopted as the working definition, since the user confirmed the full set
   "based on school crm.md":
   - **Principal:** read-only — complete school dashboard, student statistics, career progress,
     global education, reports.
   - **School Coordinator:** write access — add/manage students (including bulk, see #2 below),
     schedule activities, track attendance, monitor services, generate reports.
   - **Teacher** (net-new role, distinctly named `school_teacher` — **not** `DEC-ROLE-002`'s
     Trainer/Teacher, which remains EduSphere's own IT-instructor role, unrelated): view assigned
     students, attendance, career activities, student progress. Assigned-only, not full-school.
   - **Parent** (net-new role, `school_parent`): view their own child's/children's profile and
     progress only.
2. **Write access / bulk upload — RESOLVED: yes, template-download-first.** User confirmed:
   "Yes, template-download-first bulk upload." School Coordinator's write access includes bulk
   student-roster upload via download-template → fill offline → upload → server-side validation →
   row-level accept/reject report. This resolves the roster-upload half of `DEC-SCOPE-010` — see
   that decision's own entry for the formal cross-reference. The actor is now the **School
   Coordinator** (a school-side role), not the internal-staff-only actor this decision's own
   Recommendation section had proposed before the role-structure question was answered — that
   proposal is superseded by this resolution.
3. **Teacher login — RESOLVED: in scope**, as part of the full role set adopted in #1. Built as a
   distinctly-named `school_teacher` role specifically to avoid the `DEC-ROLE-002` collision flagged
   in the original question.
4. **Parent login — RESOLVED: in scope**, as part of the full role set adopted in #1, as
   `school_parent`.

**Cross-institution isolation confirmed:** "they can access the respective school data only" — all
four roles are scoped server-side to their own institution, the same own-institution IDOR-protection
class already confirmed for `university_rep` (`UNI-001-AC02`) and Agent (`AGT-002-AC02`). A Teacher
or Parent additionally scopes *within* their own institution to their assigned students / own
child(ren) respectively — never the whole school's roster.

**Not resolved by this decision:** whether Career Guidance, Psychometric, Counselling, or any other
substantive school-service *module* exists as buildable scope — only the **login/RBAC structure**
and the **roster bulk-upload mechanism** are confirmed here. `EVID-014`'s content for those modules
remains unconfirmed `DERIVED_BLUEPRINT` — see `DEC-SCOPE-010`'s still-open results-module
sub-question and the parallel note under item 71/`DEC-ROLE-004`'s extension below.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-14. BRD/PRD/
Feature Catalogue/RBAC propagation is follow-up work, not yet done as of this decision's resolution.

### DEC-SCOPE-012 — School partner record and role-account provisioning mechanism

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — resolved 2026-09-14, asked directly, in-session.

**Trigger:** While propagating `DEC-SCOPE-011` into `RBAC_MATRIX.md`, `DATA_MODEL.md`, and the UX
Screen Catalogue, the same gap surfaced repeatedly and was flagged every time rather than guessed at:
nothing anywhere specifies *how* a School partner record, or any of its four role accounts
(Principal/Coordinator/Teacher/Parent), actually comes into existence. `SCH-001`'s own precondition
is just "a School partner record exists" — the same placeholder phrasing `UNI-001` uses for
University Representative, which itself never resolved *how* a University partner record gets
created either. Without an answer, `SCH-001`/`SCH-002` have no onboarding path in front of them, and
a natural companion Feature ID (`SCH-003`, onboarding) cannot be authored.

**Question, in four parts — do not answer as one bundle:**

1. **Who initiates the School partnership record?** Three shapes, each with a precedent already in
   this project: (a) the School self-registers, then is gated behind an approval step — the Agent
   pattern (`DEC-SCOPE-004`: self-register, held Pending until Overseas Admin approves); (b)
   EduSphere staff creates the record entirely, with no School-side self-service step at all — the
   University Representative pattern (`UNI-001`, precondition "a University partner record exists,"
   implicitly Admin/Counselor-created, no approval gate modeled); (c) a sales/partnership-manager-led
   process — raised only in `EVID-020` (`University Partnership CRM.md`), an unattributed
   `DERIVED_BLUEPRINT` already flagged in `CONFLICT_MATRIX.md` `C-10` as proposing a role
   (Partnership Manager) with zero supporting evidence — do not treat this as a real precedent
   without its own separate confirmation.
2. **Once the School record exists, who creates the four role accounts?** Options include: EduSphere
   staff creates all four manually (consistent with `DEC-SCOPE-009`'s original "counselor-managed"
   spirit, now `DEC-ROLE-005`'s Counselor-manages-service-delivery-data framing); EduSphere creates
   one seed account (e.g. the Coordinator) which then invites/creates the other three, mirroring
   `EVID-014`'s own "School Master creates Teacher/Parent logins" vision — that document alone is not
   sufficient grounds per `CLAUDE.md`, but the shape is a legitimate option to consider; or a
   pure invite-link flow with no seed account at all.
3. **Is there an approval gate before a new School or account becomes active** — Agent's
   Pending→Approved model (`DEC-SCOPE-004`), or immediate activation like University Representative
   appears to have (no gate modeled anywhere for that role either)?
4. **Does this interact with the still-open School Partnership tiers question** (Bronze/Silver/Gold/
   Platinum, `EVID-013`, explicitly left open by the user for the real client — see
   `PRODUCT_DECISION_REGISTER.md`'s "Open, not resolved — School Partnership tiers" entry above and
   `CLIENT_QUESTIONS.md` Round 2 item 9)? E.g. does a lower partnership tier get fewer of the four
   roles, or no bulk-upload capability? **Flagged for awareness only — this question does not need
   to be answered here; a "no interaction, tiers are purely commercial" answer is a valid resolution
   to part 4 on its own.**

**Evidence:** None directly — this is a genuine gap, not a conflict between sources. The closest
analogs (`DEC-SCOPE-004` Agent self-service, `UNI-001` University Representative Admin-created) are
cited above as precedent shapes, not as answers already given for School.

**Recommendation:** The University Representative pattern (EduSphere/Admin creates the partner
record and initial account) is the closer analog — School partnerships, like University
partnerships, are established through an offline relationship (sales, BDM, or counselor contact) per
every piece of evidence touching School at all (`EVID-011`–`014`), not a self-service signup a school
administrator would stumble onto directly, unlike Agent (an individual professional registering
themselves). This is a recommendation only, not self-approving per `APPROVAL_GATES.md`.

**Resolution, 2026-09-14, asked directly across all four parts in one pass:**

1. **Record initiation — RESOLVED: EduSphere/Admin creates it entirely.** User confirmed the
   recommended option: "EduSphere/Admin creates it entirely." No School-side self-service step
   exists — matches the `UNI-001` (University Representative) pattern, not Agent's self-registration
   pattern. Given School's proposed (not yet confirmed, `RBAC_MATRIX.md` §1, item 72) `overseas`
   division assignment, the natural actor is **Overseas Admin** — same actor that manages University
   partner records and approves Agent registrations.
2. **Account creation — RESOLVED: EduSphere seeds one account, which invites the rest.** User
   confirmed this option, described as "EduSphere creates one account (e.g. Coordinator), who then
   invites/creates the other three." **The seed role is the School Coordinator** — the only
   write-capable school-side role, and the one option's own example named. Overseas Admin creates the
   School record and the seed Coordinator account together; the Coordinator then invites/creates
   Principal, Teacher, and Parent accounts for their own institution only.
3. **Activation gate — RESOLVED: no gate, active immediately.** User confirmed the recommended
   option. Both the School record and every account created under it (Coordinator by Overseas Admin;
   Principal/Teacher/Parent by the Coordinator) are active on creation — no Pending state, no
   approval action, unlike Agent's model.
4. **Partnership-tier interaction — RESOLVED: no interaction.** User confirmed: provisioning is
   independent of the still-open School Partnership tiers question (`CLIENT_QUESTIONS.md` Round 2
   item 9, which remains open on its own, unaffected by this resolution). Every School gets the same
   four-role provisioning path regardless of commercial tier, unless a future decision says
   otherwise.

**New Feature ID authorized:** `SCH-003` — School partner onboarding. See `docs/features/
feature_catalog.json`/`MASTER_FEATURE_CATALOG.md` for the propagated entry (added same session,
following this resolution).

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-14. BRD/PRD/
Feature Catalogue/RBAC/DATA_MODEL/UX propagation is follow-up work; BRD/PRD/Feature Catalogue done
this same session, `RBAC_MATRIX.md`/`DATA_MODEL.md`/UX Screen Catalogue not yet done.

### DEC-ROLE-004 — Student login scope: EduSphere-direct students only *(added on review, direct
user statement, not from a `docs/sources/` file)*

**Question:** Do School-affiliated students and Agent-referred students each get their own
individual Student login, or is a Student login issued only to a student who is EduSphere's own
direct student (self-registered/directly enrolled, not routed through a School or Agent)?

**Evidence:** A direct statement from the user in this session (2026-09-14): *"ignore the student
login for school students and agent students. Only edusphere students should have the login."* This
is consistent with — and now closes — two things already on record: (a) `DEC-SCOPE-009`'s confirmed
"not individual student logins" for School students, and (b) `EVID-011`'s own transcript detail
already summarized in `EVIDENCE_REGISTER.md` as "no agent-referred-student login," which had never
been carried into a Decision ID. It directly resolves `CLIENT_QUESTIONS.md` Round 3 item 11 (the
`C-09` conflict against `EVID-014`'s individual-login proposal) — that document's School Master →
Teacher/Parent/Student login hierarchy is rejected outright, not just for bulk upload.

**Impact — this reverses part of already-shipped, `COMPLETE` functionality, not just future scope:**
`AGT-002` ("Referred-student roster and status," `RTM.md`: complete since Wave 3, 2026-09-03) and
its data model (`DATA_MODEL.md` §6.8 `AgentStudent.student_user_id`) currently assume an
agent-referred student **has their own real `User` account** that they registered and logged into
themselves — the Agent merely views that account's roster/status. `DEC-ROLE-001` ("same Student
identity, extended," `CONFIRMED_CURRENT` 2026-09-01) unified the domestic-Student and
Overseas-applicant identity into one account without distinguishing self-registered from
agent-referred applicants, implicitly assuming every Overseas applicant — agent-referred or not —
holds their own login. Neither of those decisions is being silently overridden here; both are now
flagged as needing reconciliation against this one before any RBAC/data-model change is made.

**What this decision resolves:** no School-affiliated or Agent-referred student is issued an
individual login. Only a student who registers/enrolls with EduSphere directly gets the Student
role/account.

**On-behalf-of actor — RESOLVED same session (2026-09-14), asked directly:** if an agent-referred
student never logs in, who performs the actions currently modeled as that student's own actions in
the Overseas application journey (document upload, application submission, offer/visa status
tracking)? **User confirmed: the Agent (for their referred students) / the School representative
(for school-affiliated students) performs these on the student's behalf** — the direct analogue of
`DEC-SCOPE-009`'s counselor-managed School model, now extended explicitly to the Agent side too.

**What remains open — implementation reconciliation, not a further decision:** this changes
`AGT-002`'s already-shipped scope from a **read-only** roster/status view into a **write-capable,
on-behalf-of** workflow (document upload, submission, status updates performed by the Agent), and
touches the Overseas application state machine (`DEC-WF-001`), `DEC-ROLE-001`'s "same identity,
extended" model (an agent-referred student may now have no `User` account at all, rather than a
Student account with Overseas fields), `DEC-SCOPE-005`'s commission-trigger mechanics (student-joined
event — now presumably recorded by the Agent or an Admin, not the student), and
`docs/architecture/RBAC_MATRIX.md`/`docs/architecture/DATA_MODEL.md`, none of which have been edited
yet. This is a `docs/features/feature_catalog.json`/RBAC/data-model rework item for a future pass,
not code to change now — flag it before that pass so `AGT-002` and `DEC-ROLE-001` aren't silently
left contradicting this decision.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT (login-issuance scope **and** on-behalf-of actor model) — Approved by:
user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-14.

**Downstream documentation reconciliation — DONE, 2026-09-14 (docs only, no code/schema changed):**
per `CHANGE_CONTROL.md`'s impact-check step, the following were updated to flag (not yet build) the
consequences of this decision:
- `RBAC_MATRIX.md` §2.7/§2.8/§3 — narrowed the `overseas_student` "Self" row to self-authenticating
  applicants only; recorded the Agent's new (undesigned) on-behalf-of write need against §3's own
  "Support-staff impersonation: none — not designed" line, naming `DEC-ROLE-004` as that line's
  required Decision ID.
- `DATA_MODEL.md` §6.2/§6.8 — flagged that `student_user_id` no longer universally means a
  self-authenticating account; left the exact schema mechanism (agent-created no-login `User` row
  vs. a nullable FK with fields carried on `AgentStudent`) as an open architecture choice, not
  decided here.
- `ROLE_ACTION_MATRIX.md` — added the Agent's not-yet-built on-behalf-of grant, narrowed the
  `overseas_student` identity note, and recorded `counselor` as the anticipated (already-shaped, not
  yet formally extended) actor for `DEC-SCOPE-009`'s School-affiliated case.
- `feature_catalog.json`/`MASTER_FEATURE_CATALOG.md` — `AGT-002` and `OVS-002`/`004`/`005` each
  annotated as known-incomplete against this decision, without changing their `CURRENT`/`COMPLETE`
  status (their self-service scope is unaffected and still correct).
- `IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` — flagged that Wave 3's effort figure does not yet include the new
  on-behalf-of capability, pending a numbered Feature ID.

**Still outstanding (actual design/build work, not documentation):** a new Feature ID for the
Agent's on-behalf-of write path; the `student_user_id` schema-mechanism choice; the audit design
ensuring every on-behalf-of write is attributable to the acting Agent; and the `IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md`
delta calculation once that Feature ID exists.

### DEC-ROLE-005 — Internal EduSphere actor for school-affiliated student data management

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — resolved 2026-09-14, asked directly, in-session.

**Trigger:** `DEC-SCOPE-009`'s resolution text says "counselors manage the underlying student data
from the internal system" — generic wording. An earlier propagation pass this session incorrectly
formalized that into "the existing `Counselor` role" (scoped to Overseas applications/visas/
documents), an unwarranted inference the user caught by asking for `EVID-014`'s full content to be
re-checked — see `docs/product/PRD_OPEN_ITEMS.md` item 71 and `docs/decisions/CLIENT_QUESTIONS.md`
Round 3 item 14 for the full correction trail.

**Question:** Which internal EduSphere role(s) manage school-affiliated student data (career
guidance, psychometric, counselling, and whatever else that data eventually covers) — the existing
`Counselor` role, new specialized roles (`EVID-014` §13 proposes Career Counselor, Psychometric
Team, Academic Team, School Partnership Manager), or something else?

**Resolution:** User confirmed: "Reuse the existing Counselor role." The existing `counselor` role
(`ROLE_ACTION_MATRIX.md`'s Overseas Division `Counselor` section) is extended to also cover
school-affiliated student case management, rather than building new specialized roles. This is
simpler — one actor, not four — at the cost of less precision than `EVID-014`'s specialized-team
model; if that trade-off proves wrong once actual school-service modules (career guidance,
psychometric, etc.) are designed, it can be revisited then.

**Division of labor with `DEC-SCOPE-011`'s School Coordinator, stated explicitly to avoid
confusion:** the School Coordinator (school-side, external) manages **roster/operational** data —
creating and maintaining student records, scheduling activities, tracking attendance, per
`DEC-SCOPE-011`. The Counselor (EduSphere-side, internal, this decision) manages **service-delivery**
data — the substantive content of whatever career-guidance/psychometric/counselling work is
eventually built. These are not in conflict; they cover different halves of the data.

**Not resolved by this decision:** whether Career Guidance, Psychometric, or Counselling exist as
buildable modules at all — that remains open, same as `DEC-SCOPE-010`'s results-module sub-question.
This decision only settles *who* would manage that data once/if such modules are built.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-14. BRD/PRD/
Feature Catalogue/RBAC propagation is follow-up work, not yet done as of this decision's resolution.

**Superseded, 2026-09-14 (later, same session) — see `DEC-ROLE-006`.** This decision's own text
flagged the trade-off explicitly: "if that trade-off proves wrong once actual school-service modules
... are designed, it can be revisited then." Once results/career-guidance/psychometric/counselling
were confirmed in scope (`DEC-ROLE-006`), the user reopened this and adopted the five-specialized-
role model instead. This entry is kept, not deleted, per this project's traceability convention —
`DEC-ROLE-006` is now the governing decision for internal School-domain actors.

### DEC-ROLE-006 — Five specialized internal School-domain roles; results/career-guidance/
psychometric/counselling module scope

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — resolved 2026-09-14, asked directly, in-session.

**Trigger:** The user asked to decide two remaining School Portal gaps in one pass: (1) whether an
academic results/marks module is in scope (`DEC-SCOPE-010` part 2, left open earlier the same
session), and (2) whether Career Guidance, Psychometric, and Counselling exist as buildable modules
at all (left open by `DEC-ROLE-005`).

**Resolution — module scope:**

1. **Academic results/marks module — CONFIRMED in scope.** User confirmed directly ("Yes, in
   scope").
2. **Career Guidance, Psychometric, and Counselling — CONFIRMED in scope, all three.** User
   confirmed directly ("Yes, all three").

**Resolution — internal actor model, superseding `DEC-ROLE-005`'s "reuse the existing Counselor
role":** asked who uploads results and what gate applies, the user answered by actor-responsibility
rather than picking a single role: **"based on team responsibility — Psychometric Team, Career
Counselor, Edusphere School Manager, Academic Team, School Partnership Manager."** This names, and
this project now adopts, `EVID-014` §13's five specialized roles verbatim, in place of the single
extended `Counselor` role `DEC-ROLE-005` originally confirmed:

- **Academic Team** — owns the results/marks module: uploads results, views academic progress.
- **Career Counselor** — owns Career Guidance and Counselling (treated as one combined domain,
  matching `EVID-014`'s own description of this role: conducts career guidance, adds counselling
  notes, uploads recommendations).
- **Psychometric Team** — owns the Psychometric module: assigns assessments, uploads reports, views
  psychometric data.
- **Edusphere School Manager** — manages a portfolio of assigned schools. **Exact duties beyond
  `EVID-014`'s one-line description are OPEN** — not itemized in this exchange, do not invent beyond
  "manage assigned schools."
- **School Partnership Manager** — manages the school relationship, views school-level reports,
  schedules meetings, manages activities. **Overlap with Overseas Admin's `SCH-003` onboarding
  duties and with "Edusphere School Manager" above is OPEN, not reconciled here** — do not assume
  this role replaces Overseas Admin's provisioning role or how it divides from Edusphere School
  Manager; flag for Contracts-phase confirmation before building either of these two specifically.

None of these five is the existing `Counselor` role (`DEC-ROLE-005`'s original choice) — they are
net-new, EduSphere-internal roles, distinct from the existing Overseas `Counselor`, and also
distinct from the School-side `school_coordinator`/`school_principal`/`school_teacher`/
`school_parent` roles (`DEC-SCOPE-011`).

**Division of labor with School Coordinator, resolved in the same pass:** asked whether School
Coordinator gets any visibility into this content, user confirmed: **Coordinator gets read-only
visibility/reporting** — the five specialized teams above exclusively create/manage the substantive
content (results, career guidance, psychometric, counselling); Coordinator can see summary
status/reports for their own school, but never creates or edits this content directly. This
preserves `DEC-ROLE-005`'s original division-of-labor principle (Coordinator = roster/operational
data; internal staff = service-delivery data) — only *which* internal staff changed.

**Results approval gate — adopted, one detail not verbatim-confirmed:** `EVID-014`'s
Draft→Verified→Published gate is adopted as the working design for the results module, as the
natural pairing with a team-responsibility model (preventing an incomplete or unverified Academic
Team entry from reaching Parent/Student). This specific mechanism was not independently re-confirmed
word-for-word in this exchange — flagged so it can be corrected quickly if wrong, not treated as
equally solid as the other resolutions in this entry.

**New Feature IDs authorized:** `SCH-004` (Career Guidance & Counselling), `SCH-005` (Psychometric
Assessment), `SCH-006` (Academic Results, with the Draft→Verified→Published gate). See
`docs/features/feature_catalog.json`/`MASTER_FEATURE_CATALOG.md` for the propagated entries.

**Evidence:** `EVID-014` §13 (an unattributed `DERIVED_BLUEPRINT` — its role list is not itself
authoritative; it becomes governing only because the user directly named and confirmed it here, per
`CLAUDE.md`'s rule that a derived document's content requires explicit user/client confirmation, not
the document's own claims).

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-14.
Supersedes `DEC-ROLE-005`'s actor choice; resolves `DEC-SCOPE-010` part 2 jointly. Open, not decided
here: Edusphere School Manager's and School Partnership Manager's exact duties/overlap, and the
Draft→Verified→Published gate's exact re-confirmation. BRD/PRD/Feature Catalogue/RBAC/DATA_MODEL/UX
propagation is follow-up work, not yet done as of this decision's resolution.

### DEC-SCOPE-013 — Assignment-scope mechanism for Academic Team / Career Counselor / Psychometric Team

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — resolved 2026-09-14, asked directly, in-session.

**Trigger:** While propagating `DEC-ROLE-006` into `RBAC_MATRIX.md` (§2.12) and `DATA_MODEL.md`
(§6.14, §6.16–6.18), every one of the three specialized roles' grant rows had to be written as
"assigned school-affiliated students only — scope mechanism not yet designed, see §5," because
nothing in `DEC-ROLE-006` or `EVID-014` specifies *how* a member of `academic_team`/
`career_counselor`/`psychometric_team` becomes scoped to a particular set of students. Without an
answer, none of `SCH-004`/`005`/`006`'s core RBAC enforcement can be built — this is the single
largest blocker to those three features' coding-readiness (`RTM.md`'s School section, `SCH-CAREER-01`/
`SCH-PSYCH-01` stubs are explicitly partial because of it).

**Question:** How is a member of one of these three roles scoped to "their assigned students"? Three
shapes, each with a precedent already in this project:

1. **Per-school portfolio** — the role is assigned to one or more entire *institutions*, and can act
   on any student at any school in their portfolio (mirrors `edusphere_school_manager`'s own framing,
   "manages assigned schools," and Trainer's own-batch-at-the-batch-level pattern generalized to
   institution level).
2. **Per-student assignment** — the role is assigned individual students directly, one at a time or
   in batches, regardless of institution (mirrors `school_teacher`'s own proposed per-student
   assignment mechanism, `DATA_MODEL.md` §6.12, and Counselor's existing `CNS-001` caseload pattern).
3. **Per-institution blanket access** — any member of the role can act on any school-affiliated
   student at any institution, with no individual assignment step at all (simplest to build, but the
   weakest least-privilege posture of the three — a member with no relationship to a specific school
   could still act on its students).

**Evidence:** None directly — `EVID-014` names the five roles' responsibilities but never describes
an assignment mechanism for any of them; this is a genuine gap, not a conflict between sources.

**Recommendation:** Option 1 (per-school portfolio) is the closer fit for how these three roles are
described — "Academic Team," "Career Counselor," and "Psychometric Team" read as small internal teams
each covering a set of partner schools, not as caseworkers assigned to hand-picked individual
students the way a Counselor is. It also reuses `edusphere_school_manager`'s own stated "assigned
schools" framing rather than inventing a second, different assignment shape for a closely related set
of roles. This is a recommendation only, not self-approving per `APPROVAL_GATES.md`.

**Resolution, 2026-09-14:** **Option 1 — per-school portfolio, RESOLVED.** User confirmed the
recommended option. Each `academic_team`/`career_counselor`/`psychometric_team` member is assigned
one or more entire `School` institutions and may act on any school-affiliated student at any
institution in their own portfolio — never a student at a school outside it, even via a direct record
ID. This requires a portfolio-membership table (e.g. `SchoolStaffAssignment(user_id, school_id)`,
many-to-many — one member can cover several schools, one school can have several members of the same
role) rather than a per-student FK; the exact schema is Contracts-phase detail, not fixed by this
decision. Propagation into `RBAC_MATRIX.md`/`DATA_MODEL.md` is follow-up work, not yet done as of
this resolution.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-14.

### DEC-SCOPE-014 — Account provisioning for Academic Team / Career Counselor / Psychometric Team

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — resolved 2026-09-14, asked directly, in-session.

**Trigger:** `SCH-003`'s onboarding flow (`DEC-SCOPE-012`) covers only the four school-side roles
(Principal/Coordinator/Teacher/Parent) — Overseas Admin creates the School + seed Coordinator, and
the Coordinator invites the other three. Nothing anywhere specifies how `academic_team`/
`career_counselor`/`psychometric_team` accounts — internal EduSphere staff, not school-side actors —
come into existence. `DATA_MODEL.md` §6.15 explicitly notes `SchoolAccountInvite` "provisions the
four school-side accounts only" and flags this as a separate, undesigned question.

**Question:** How is an `academic_team`/`career_counselor`/`psychometric_team` account created? Three
shapes, each with a precedent already in this project:

1. **Same pattern as other internal staff roles** (`it_admin`, `overseas_admin`, `counselor`,
   `placement_team`, `hr_team`) — created directly by a Super Admin or an existing division Admin
   through the Admin console's user-management surface (`ADM-001`/`004`), no invite-token flow at
   all. Simplest, and consistent with how every other *internal* (non-external-partner) role in this
   project gets its account.
2. **Extend `SCH-003`'s invite flow** to a fourth invitable role value, issued by Overseas Admin (or
   `edusphere_school_manager`, if `DEC-SCOPE-013`/item 75 resolves that role as the natural issuer) —
   reuses the existing `SchoolAccountInvite` mechanism rather than building a second one.
3. **A dedicated internal-staff onboarding step**, separate from both the Admin console and the
   School invite flow — only worth considering if neither of the above fits once the assignment-scope
   answer (`DEC-SCOPE-013`) is known (e.g. if portfolio assignment needs to happen *at* account
   creation, not as a separate step afterward).

**Evidence:** None directly — `EVID-014` never describes how these roles' accounts are created, only
their responsibilities once they exist.

**Recommendation:** Option 1 — these are internal EduSphere staff roles, not external school-side
actors, so the existing internal-staff account-creation pattern (Admin console) is the more consistent
fit than reusing a mechanism (`SchoolAccountInvite`) purpose-built for external partner accounts. This
is a recommendation only, not self-approving per `APPROVAL_GATES.md`.

**Resolution, 2026-09-14:** **Option 1 (Admin console pattern) confirmed, actor narrowed by direct
user answer:** "created by overseas admin or super admin." Accounts for all three roles are created
through the existing Admin console user-management surface (`ADM-001`/`004`'s pattern), **not** via
`SchoolAccountInvite` — but unlike the generic Option 1 draft (any Super Admin or division Admin),
creation is restricted to **Overseas Admin or Super Admin specifically** — `it_admin` does not create
these accounts, consistent with School's proposed `overseas` division assignment (`RBAC_MATRIX.md`
§1, still itself not independently confirmed — see `PRD_OPEN_ITEMS.md` item 72). This is narrower
than Option 1's original wording, not a different option — recorded as the user's own refinement, not
inferred. Which portfolio (per `DEC-SCOPE-013`) a newly-created account is assigned to at creation
time is Contracts-phase detail, not fixed by this decision. Propagation into `RBAC_MATRIX.md`/
`DATA_MODEL.md` is follow-up work, not yet done as of this resolution.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-14.

### DEC-ROLE-007 — Academic results Draft→Verified→Published gate: verify/publish actor

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — resolved 2026-09-14, asked directly, in-session.

**Trigger:** `DEC-ROLE-006` adopted `EVID-014`'s Draft→Verified→Published gate as the working design
for `SCH-006`, but explicitly flagged it as "not independently re-confirmed word-for-word" — the one
detail in that decision not asked about directly. While propagating into `DATA_MODEL.md` §6.16,
`SchoolAcademicResult` needed separate nullable `verified_by_user_id`/`published_by_user_id` columns
specifically because who performs those two steps is unresolved, and `RBAC_MATRIX.md` §2.12 currently
grants all three transitions to `academic_team` alone "as the simplest reading," flagged for revision
once this is answered.

**Question, two parts:**

1. **Does `academic_team` perform all three transitions itself** (upload → verify → publish, one
   role throughout), **or does a different actor verify/publish** — e.g. a second `academic_team`
   member (peer review), or a different role entirely such as `edusphere_school_manager` or
   `school_coordinator` (a supervisory sign-off before the result reaches a family)?
2. **If a different `academic_team` member specifically** (not a different role): must it be
   demonstrably a *different* individual than the uploader (a same-actor restriction, the same shape
   already confirmed for Agent commission payouts, `RBAC_MATRIX.md` §2.8), or may the same person
   upload, verify, and publish their own entry?

**Evidence:** `EVID-014` describes the gate's existence (Draft/Verified/Published as states a result
passes through) but not who performs each transition — this is a genuine gap in that source, not a
conflict.

**Recommendation:** A same-actor restriction (part 2, mirroring the Agent commission payout pattern
already confirmed in this project) is the more defensible default for anything reaching a family as
an official academic record — but *who* the second actor is (another Academic Team member vs. a
different role) is a genuine open choice this document does not recommend one way over the other,
since either is plausible from the role names alone. This is a recommendation only, not
self-approving per `APPROVAL_GATES.md`.

**Resolution, 2026-09-14, both parts resolved in one answer:** User confirmed the recommended option:
**a different `academic_team` member must verify/publish** — not a different role, and not the same
uploader. This resolves both parts of the question together:

1. **Actor — RESOLVED: `academic_team`, not a different role.** Verify/publish stays within
   `academic_team` — `edusphere_school_manager`/`school_coordinator` play no role in this gate.
2. **Same-actor restriction — RESOLVED: yes, mandatory.** The `academic_team` member who uploaded a
   result (`uploaded_by_user_id`) may never be the same identity recorded as `verified_by_user_id` or
   `published_by_user_id` — enforced server-side, same two-gate shape as `AGT-004`'s commission-payout
   same-actor restriction (`RBAC_MATRIX.md` §2.8, `NFR-SEC-002`). A single-member `academic_team`
   portfolio (per `DEC-SCOPE-013`) would therefore be unable to ever publish its own results —
   **flagged as a real operational consequence of this resolution**, not silently absorbed: a
   portfolio needs at least two `academic_team` members, or verify/publish blocks permanently. Not
   re-litigated here since the user confirmed the restriction directly; carried forward as an
   implementation-readiness note.

Also resolves `PRD_OPEN_ITEMS.md` item 74's remaining open half — the Draft→Verified→Published gate
itself (already adopted per `DEC-ROLE-006`) is now fully confirmed, including who performs each step.

**Status:** CONFIRMED_CURRENT — Approved by: user (in-session) — Approval date: 2026-09-14.
Propagation into `RBAC_MATRIX.md`/`DATA_MODEL.md` is follow-up work, not yet done as of this
resolution.
